Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any differential module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 234. The projective resolution has length at most 449 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_234(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 234. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^449(S, F) is trivial. QED.
